function restoreShell(){if(shellRelease)document.documentElement.dataset.cvSocial=shellRelease;if(shellStyle&&!$('style[data-cv-social]'))document.head.append(shellStyle.cloneNode(true));if(shellHeader&&!$('#cv-social-header')){document.body.prepend(shellHeader.cloneNode(true));applyHeader()}$$('#cv-shared-header,.cv-shared-header,.top,#cv-marketplace-nav').forEach(n=>{if(!n.hidden)n.hidden=true})}
function optimizeRebuiltImages(root){if(root.nodeType!==Node.ELEMENT_NODE)return;const nodes=root.matches('img,video')?[root]:[...root.querySelectorAll('img,video')];for(const n of nodes){const attr=n.matches('video')?'poster':'src',a=imageMap[n.getAttribute(attr)];if(!a)continue;n.setAttribute(attr,a.variants[1].url);if(n.matches('img')){n.setAttribute('srcset',a.variants.filter((v,i,x)=>x.findIndex(z=>z.width===v.width)===i).map(v=>v.url+' '+v.width+'w').join(','));n.setAttribute('sizes','(max-width:600px) 50vw,25vw');n.setAttribute('decoding','async')}}}

function imageAttrs(src,sizes='(max-width:600px) 50vw,25vw'){const a=imageMap[src];if(!a&&/^\/communiverse\/_public\/media\/images\/20261008\/[a-f0-9]{24}-(320|720|1280)\.webp$/.test(src))return 'src="'+esc(src)+'" srcset="'+[320,720,1280].map(w=>esc(src.replace(/-(320|720|1280)\.webp$/,'-'+w+'.webp'))+' '+w+'w').join(',')+'" sizes="'+sizes+'" decoding="async"';return a?'src="'+esc(a.variants[1].url)+'" srcset="'+esc(a.variants.filter((v,n,list)=>list.findIndex(x=>x.width===v.width)===n).map(v=>v.url+' '+v.width+'w').join(','))+'" sizes="'+sizes+'" width="'+a.variants[1].width+'" height="'+a.variants[1].height+'" decoding="async"':'src="'+esc(src)+'"'}
function artistLinks(a){return [...(a.website?[{label:'Website',url:a.website}]:[]),...(Array.isArray(a.socials)?a.socials:[])].filter(link=>{try{return new URL(link.url).protocol==='https:'}catch{return false}}).map(link=>'<a class="cv-text-link" href="'+esc(link.url)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">'+esc(link.label)+'</a>').join('');}
const localSource=i=>i.kind==='event'||i.kind==='venue'?'/communiverse/event/?id='+encodeURIComponent(i.id):i.gallery?'/communiverse/gallery/?id='+encodeURIComponent(i.gallery.id):i.source?.startsWith('https:')?'/communiverse/reference/?url='+encodeURIComponent(i.source):i.source||'/communiverse/';
function notify(message){let t=$('#cv-social-toast');if(!t){t=document.createElement('div');t.id='cv-social-toast';t.setAttribute('role','status');document.body.append(t)}t.innerHTML=message+' <button type="button" data-dismiss-toast aria-label="Dismiss message">×</button>';t.hidden=false;}

function tile(i,omitMedia=false){items.set(i.id,i);return `<article class="cv-pin" data-id="${esc(i.id)}"><button class="cv-pin-media" data-open="${esc(i.id)}" aria-label="Explore ${esc(i.title)}">${i.video&&!omitMedia?`<video data-film="${esc(i.video)}" poster="${esc(cached(i.image))}" autoplay muted loop playsinline preload="none" aria-label="${esc(i.title)}"></video>`:i.image&&!omitMedia?`<img ${imageAttrs(i.image)} alt="${esc(i.title)}" loading="lazy" decoding="async" referrerpolicy="no-referrer">`:`<span class="cv-pin-text">${esc(i.title)}</span>`}</button><button class="cv-heart${i.liked?' is-active':''}" data-heart="${esc(i.id)}" aria-pressed="${!!i.liked}" aria-label="${i.liked?'Remove':'Save'} ${esc(i.title)}">${heart}<span data-heart-count>${i.hearts||0}</span></button><div class="cv-pin-caption"><button data-open="${esc(i.id)}">${esc(i.title)}</button><a href="/communiverse/artist/?id=${esc(i.artist||'')}">${esc(i.artistName||i.organizer||'Community')}</a></div></article>`;}

function distribute(root){if(!root)return;const columns=[...root.children].filter(c=>c.classList.contains('cv-feed-column'));for(const col of columns){while(col.firstChild)root.insertBefore(col.firstChild,col);col.remove()}root.classList.add('cv-laid-out');const cards=[...root.children].filter(c=>c.matches('.cv-pin,.cv-artist-pin'));for(const card of cards){const h=card.getBoundingClientRect().height;card.style.gridRowEnd='span '+Math.ceil((h+16)/8);if(!card.dataset.cvResize){card.dataset.cvResize='1';cardResize.observe(card)}}}
const cardResize=new ResizeObserver(entries=>{for(const {target:card}of entries){card.style.gridRowEnd='span '+Math.ceil((card.getBoundingClientRect().height+16)/8)}});
const masonryObserver=new ResizeObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(({target:r})=>{const width=r.clientWidth;if(r.dataset.masonryWidth!==String(width)){r.dataset.masonryWidth=width;distribute(r)}}));function masonry(){$$('.cv-masonry').forEach(r=>{distribute(r);masonryObserver.observe(r)})}
function applyHeader(){if(!me||$('#cv-workspace'))return;const a=$('#cv-social-header .cv-account');if(a)a.innerHTML=`<a href="/communiverse/workspace/" class="cv-profile-chip"><span class="cv-avatar"><img src="${esc(me.member.photo||'/communiverse/_public/avatar/member-'+me.member.id)}" alt="" width="36" height="36"></span><span>${esc(me.member.first_name)}</span></a>`;$$('a[href="/communiverse/join/"]').filter(a=>!a.closest('form')).forEach(a=>{a.href='/communiverse/workspace/';a.textContent='My workspace'});}
async function loadMe(){const d=await api('me');me=d.member?d:null;applyHeader();const back=me&&circleReturn();if(back&&['/communiverse/profile/','/communiverse/sign-in/','/communiverse/join/','/communiverse/onboarding/'].includes(location.pathname)){try{localStorage.removeItem('cv-circle-return')}catch{}location.assign(back)}return d}
const header=$('#cv-social-header');if(header)new ResizeObserver(()=>document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--cv-social-header-height',header.getBoundingClientRect().height+'px')).observe(header);
function updateHeart(i){items.set(i.id,i);$$('[data-heart]').filter(b=>b.dataset.heart===i.id).forEach(b=>{b.classList.toggle('is-active',i.liked);b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(i.liked));b.setAttribute('aria-label',(i.liked?'Remove':'Save')+' '+i.title);b.querySelector('[data-heart-count]').textContent=i.hearts;});}
async function toggleHeart(b){if(!me){notify('Keep what moves you. <a href="/communiverse/sign-in/">Sign in</a> · <a href="/communiverse/join/?intent=collector">Join as a collector</a>');return;}const id=b.dataset.heart,i=items.get(id);if(!i)return;const target=$('#cv-work-status')||$('#cv-feed-status');b.disabled=true;try{const d=await api('hearts',{id,active:!i.liked});updateHeart(d.items[0]);status(target,d.items[0].liked?'Saved to your collection. Your next feed will reflect this.':'Removed from your collection.');notify(d.items[0].liked?'Saved to your collection.':'Removed from your collection.');}catch(e){error(target,e)}finally{b.disabled=false}}
async function follow(b){if(!me){if(b.closest('.cv-artist-pin'))openArtistPreview(b.dataset.follow,true,b);notify('Sign in to follow artists. <a href="/communiverse/sign-in/">Sign in</a>');return}b.disabled=true;try{const d=await api('follows',{id:b.dataset.follow,active:b.getAttribute('aria-pressed')!=='true'});const a=artistRecords.find(a=>a.id===b.dataset.follow);if(a)a.following=d.following;$$('[data-follow]').filter(x=>x.dataset.follow===b.dataset.follow).forEach(x=>{x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(d.following));x.innerHTML=plus;const name=x.dataset.personName||'this artist';x.setAttribute('aria-label',(d.following?'Remove ':'Add ')+name+(d.following?' from':' to')+' my circle');x.title=d.following?'In your circle':'Keep in your circle';const note=x.nextElementSibling;if(note?.classList.contains('cv-circle-note'))note.textContent=d.following?'In your circle':'Keep in your circle'});await loadMe();const origin=b.closest('.cv-inline-story')?.dataset.inlineArtist;if(origin)await openArtistPreview(origin,false,b);else if(b.closest('.cv-artist-pin'))await openArtistPreview(b.dataset.follow,false,b);}catch(e){error($('#cv-work-status')||$('#cv-artist-status')||$('#cv-feed-status'),e)}finally{b.disabled=false}}
function feedQuery(){const q=new URLSearchParams({q:$('#cv-feed-search')?.value||'',region:$('#cv-feed-region')?.value||''});const gallery=new URL(location.href).searchParams.get('gallery');if(gallery)q.set('gallery',gallery);if(view==='video')q.set('type','video');if(view==='saved')q.set('saved','1');if(view==='following')q.set('following','1');if(cursor)q.set('cursor',cursor);return q}
function feedMediaKey(i){return i?.video||cached(i?.image||'').replace(/-(320|720|1280)\.webp(?:\?.*)?$/,'-asset.webp')}
async function loadFeed(reset=false){if(busy&&!reset)return;const mine=reset?++epoch:epoch;busy=true;$('#cv-feed-more').disabled=true;status($('#cv-feed-status'),'Finding something for you…');if(reset){closeInlineWork();cursor=null;observer?.disconnect();}try{const d=await api('feed?'+feedQuery());if(mine!==epoch)return;if(reset){$('#cv-feed').innerHTML='';items.clear()}const seen=new Set($$('#cv-feed .cv-pin[data-id]').map(i=>i.dataset.id));const mediaSeen=new Set([...seen].map(id=>feedMediaKey(items.get(id))).filter(Boolean));let markup='';for(const i of d.items){if(seen.has(i.id))continue;const media=feedMediaKey(i);markup+=tile(i,!!media&&mediaSeen.has(media));if(media)mediaSeen.add(media);seen.add(i.id);if(seen.size===8&&!view.includes('saved'))markup+=invitation('collector');if(seen.size===28&&view==='all')markup+=invitation('city-representative');}$('#cv-feed').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',markup);cursor=d.cursor;$('#cv-feed-more').hidden=!cursor;status($('#cv-feed-status'),cursor?'':d.total?'You’re caught up. New works will appear as the collection grows.':'Artists and their work are coming soon.');status($('#cv-feed-note'),d.personalized?'Picked from the materials, artists and places you care about.':'A little art. A little discovery. Sign in to make it yours.');masonry();observeFeed();observeFilms();}catch(e){if(mine===epoch)error($('#cv-feed-status'),e)}finally{if(mine===epoch){busy=false;$('#cv-feed-more').disabled=false}}}
const watchedVideos=new WeakSet(),mediaPreference=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)');
const filmObserver=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(({target:v,isIntersecting})=>{v.dataset.cvVisible=String(isIntersecting);const active=isIntersecting&&!document.hidden&&!v.closest('[inert]')&&!mediaPreference.matches&&v.dataset.cvUserPaused!=='true';if(active){if(v.dataset.film&&!v.getAttribute('src'))v.src=v.dataset.film;v.play().catch(()=>{})}else v.pause();}),{threshold:.15});
function configureVideo(v){if(v.closest('#cv-chat-heads')||v.hasAttribute('data-cv-media-preview'))return;if(v.controls)v.controls=false;if(!v.autoplay)v.autoplay=true;if(!v.loop)v.loop=true;if(!v.muted)v.muted=true;if(!v.defaultMuted)v.defaultMuted=true;if(!v.playsInline)v.playsInline=true;for(const key of ['disablepictureinpicture','disableremoteplayback'])if(!v.hasAttribute(key))v.setAttribute(key,'');v.setAttribute('controlslist','nofullscreen noremoteplayback');if(!watchedVideos.has(v)){watchedVideos.add(v);filmObserver.observe(v);if(!v.closest('button')){v.tabIndex=0;v.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.code==='Space'){e.preventDefault();v.dataset.cvUserPaused=String(!v.paused);if(v.paused)v.play().catch(()=>{});else v.pause()}})}}}
function observeFilms(root=document){if(root.matches?.('video'))configureVideo(root);root.querySelectorAll?.('video').forEach(configureVideo)}
function cleanPresentation(root){if(root.nodeType===Node.TEXT_NODE){if(root.parentElement?.closest('script,style,textarea'))return;const text=cleanDisplay(root.nodeValue);if(text!==root.nodeValue)root.nodeValue=text;return}if(root.nodeType!==Node.ELEMENT_NODE&&root!==document.body)return;if(root.matches?.('script,style,textarea'))return;for(const key of ['alt','title','aria-label','placeholder']){const value=root.getAttribute?.(key);if(value!==null&&value!==undefined){const cleaned=cleanDisplay(value);if(cleaned!==value)root.setAttribute(key,cleaned)}}const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);while(walker.nextNode())cleanPresentation(walker.currentNode);root.querySelectorAll?.('[alt],[title],[aria-label],[placeholder]').forEach(el=>{for(const key of ['alt','title','aria-label','placeholder']){const value=el.getAttribute(key);if(value!==null){const cleaned=cleanDisplay(value);if(cleaned!==value)el.setAttribute(key,cleaned)}}});optimizeRebuiltImages(root);observeFilms(root)}
cleanPresentation(document.body);restoreShell();new MutationObserver(records=>{for(const r of records){if(r.type==='characterData')cleanPresentation(r.target);else if(r.type==='attributes'){if(r.attributeName==='controls'&&r.target.matches('video'))configureVideo(r.target);else cleanPresentation(r.target)}else{r.addedNodes.forEach(cleanPresentation);r.removedNodes.forEach(root=>{if(root.nodeType!==Node.ELEMENT_NODE||root.isConnected)return;const videos=root.matches('video')?[root]:root.querySelectorAll('video');videos.forEach(v=>{v.pause();filmObserver.unobserve(v);watchedVideos.delete(v)});if(root.matches('.cv-masonry'))masonryObserver.unobserve(root);root.querySelectorAll('.cv-masonry').forEach(r=>masonryObserver.unobserve(r))})}}restoreShell()}).observe(document.documentElement,{subtree:true,childList:true,characterData:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['controls','alt','title','aria-label','placeholder','src','poster']});
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{$$('video').forEach(v=>{if(document.hidden)v.pause();else if(v.dataset.cvVisible==='true'&&!v.closest('[inert]')&&!mediaPreference.matches&&v.dataset.cvUserPaused!=='true')v.play().catch(()=>{})})});mediaPreference.addEventListener('change',()=>{$$('video').forEach(v=>{if(mediaPreference.matches)v.pause();else if(v.dataset.cvVisible==='true'&&!document.hidden)v.play().catch(()=>{})})});

function observeFeed(){observer?.disconnect();if(cursor){observer=new IntersectionObserver(es=>{if(es.some(e=>e.isIntersecting)&&!busy)loadFeed()},{rootMargin:'500px'});observer.observe($('#cv-feed-sentinel'));}}
async function comments(target){try{const d=await api('comments?target='+encodeURIComponent(target));$('#cv-comments').innerHTML=d.comments.length?d.comments.map(c=>`<article class="cv-comment"><a href="/communiverse/plug/?person=${c.author}">${esc(c.first_name)}</a><p>${esc(c.body)}</p>${me&&c.memberId===me.member.id?`<button data-remove="${c.id}" data-kind="comment">Remove my comment</button>`:`<button data-report="${c.id}">Report</button>`}</article>`).join(''):'<p class="cv-muted">Start the conversation.</p>';}catch(e){status($('#cv-comments'),e.message)}}

class="cv-text-link" href="${esc(i.submittedWork?i.source:localSource(i))}" ${i.source.startsWith('https:')?'target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"':''}>${i.submittedWork?esc(i.sourceLabel||'Visit function artistPin(a){const cover=a.cover||(a.memberProfile?{}:{image:a.image,title:a.title});return `<article class="cv-artist-pin" data-artist-id="${esc(a.id)}"><button class="cv-artist-pin-media" data-open-artist="${esc(a.id)}" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Explore ${esc(a.title)}">${cover.video?`<video data-film="${esc(cover.video)}" poster="${esc(cached(cover.image))}" autoplay muted loop playsinline preload="none" aria-label="${esc(cover.title)}"></video>`:cover.image?`<img ${imageAttrs(cover.image)} alt="${esc(cover.title||a.title)}" loading="lazy">`:`<span class="cv-artist-text-card"><span class="cv-kicker">A practice to discover</span><span>${esc(a.description||'Discover their work and point of view.')}</span></span>`}</button><div class="cv-artist-pin-caption"><a href="/communiverse/artist/?id=${esc(a.id)}">${esc(a.title)}</a><p>${esc(a.city||a.organizer||'Communiverse')}</p><button class="cv-story-plus cv-artist-follow" data-follow="${esc(a.id)}" data-person-name="${esc(a.title)}" aria-pressed="${!!a.following}" aria-label="${a.following?'Unfollow':'Follow'} ${esc(a.title)}">${plus}</button></div><div class="cv-pin-story" hidden></div></article>`}
function artistSelection(){const fold=v=>v.toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,''),q=fold(($('#cv-artist-search')?.value||'').trim());return artistRecords.filter(a=>(artistMode!=='following'||a.following)&&(!q||fold([a.title,a.city,a.organizer,a.description,...a.tags].join(' ')).includes(q)))}
function renderArtistPins(reset=false){const root=$('#cv-artists');if(!root)return;artistObserver?.disconnect();if(reset){artistOffset=0;root.replaceChildren()}const rows=artistSelection(),batch=rows.slice(artistOffset,artistOffset+20);root.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',batch.map(artistPin).join(''));artistOffset+=batch.length;masonry();observeFilms(root);const more=artistOffset<rows.length;$('#cv-artists-more').hidden=!more;status($('#cv-artists-status'),rows.length?'':artistMode==='following'?'Artists you follow will appear here.':artistRecords.length?'No artists match this search. async function loadArtists(){const mine=++artistEpoch;status($('#cv-artists-status'),'Finding artists…');try{const region=$('#cv-artist-region').value,d=await api('artists?region='+encodeURIComponent(region));if(mine!==artistEpoch)return;artistRecords=d.artists;renderArtistPins(true);const id=new URL(location.href).searchParams.get('artist');if(id)openArtistPreview(id,false)}catch(e){if(mine===artistEpoch)status($('#cv-artists-status'),e.message)}}

class="cv-work-tags">${a.tags.slice(0,6).map(t=>`<span>${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="cv-work-actions">${circleButton(a)}</div><a class="cv-quiet-button"  async function load(){if(busy)return;busy=true;more.disabled=true;message.textContent='Loading work…';try{const d=await api('feed?artist='+encodeURIComponent(id)+(cursor?'&cursor='+encodeURIComponent(cursor):''));if(!host.isConnected)return;const rows=d.items.filter(i=>i.artist===id);host.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',rows.map(item=>tile(item)).join(''));shown+=rows.length;total=d.total;cursor=d.cursor;more.hidden=!cursor;message.textContent=total?shown+' of '+total+' works':'';if(!total)host.innerHTML='<p class="cv-muted">Explore the artist’s portfolio for more of their work.</p>';masonry();observeFilms(host);}catch(e){message.textContent=e.message;more.hidden=false;more.textContent='Try loading work again';}finally{busy=false;more.disabled=false;}}
 more.addEventListener('click',load);await load();
}

let selectedGallery=null,galleryView='works';
async function loadGallery(){try{const id=new URL(location.href).searchParams.get('id'),d=await api('galleries'+(id?'?id='+encodeURIComponent(id):''));if(!id){$('#cv-gallery-profile').innerHTML='<p class="cv-kicker">Spaces for discovery</p><h1>Galleries.</h1><p>Meet the spaces, artists and ideas behind the work.</p>';$('.cv-gallery-nav').hidden=true;$('#cv-gallery-content').innerHTML='<div class="cv-directory">'+d.galleries.map(g=>`<article class="cv-directory-card">${g.image?`<img ${imageAttrs(g.image)} alt="Work published by ${esc(g.name)}" loading="lazy">`:''}<p class="cv-kicker">${esc(g.city)}</p><h3><a href="/communiverse/gallery/?id=${esc(g.id)}">${esc(g.name)}</a></h3><p>${g.artists.length} artists · ${g.works.length} works</p></article>`).join('')+'</div>';return}selectedGallery=d.galleries[0];if(!selectedGallery)throw Error('This gallery is unavailable.');const g=selectedGallery;document.title=g.name+' | Communiverse';$('#cv-gallery-profile').innerHTML=`<div><p class="cv-kicker">${esc(g.city)} · Gallery</p><h1>${esc(g.name)}</h1><p>${esc(g.description)}</p><p class="cv-muted">${g.artists.length} artists · ${g.works.length} works · ${g.events.length} programmes</p><details class="cv-source-note"><summary>Source and credits</summary><p>Works and programme information are credited to ${esc(g.name)}. Availability, prices and visiting schedules should be confirmed with the venue.</p><p class="cv-source-address">${esc(g.sources[0]||'')}</p></details></div>${g.image?`<img ${imageAttrs(g.image,'(max-width:600px) 100vw,40vw')} alt="Work published by ${esc(g.name)}">`:''}`;await galleryContent()}catch(e){status($('#cv-gallery-status'),e.message)}}
async function galleryContent(){const g=selectedGallery;if(!g)return;$('#cv-gallery-content').innerHTML='<p class="cv-muted">Loading '+galleryView+'…</p>';if(galleryView==='works'){const d=await api('feed?gallery='+encodeURIComponent(g.id));$('#cv-gallery-content').innerHTML='<div class="cv-masonry">'+d.items.map(item=>tile(item)).join('')+'</div>'+(d.cursor?'<a href="/communiverse/?gallery='+g.id+'">Explore all works</a>':'');masonry();observeFilms()}else if(galleryView==='artists'){const d=await api('artists');$('#cv-gallery-content').innerHTML='<div class="cv-directory">'+d.artists.filter(a=>g.artists.includes(a.id)).map(a=>artistCard(a)).join('')+'</div>'}else{const events=[];for(const state of ['upcoming','today','places']){const d=await api('events?state='+state+'&gallery='+encodeURIComponent(g.id));events.push(...d.events.filter(e=>g.events.includes(e.id)))}$('#cv-gallery-content').innerHTML='<div class="cv-event-grid">'+events.map(eventCard).join('')+'</div>'}if(!$('#cv-gallery-content').textContent.trim())$('#cv-gallery-content').innerHTML='<p class="cv-muted">The gallery’s next update will appear here.</p>'}
